The tournament winner Azir, alongside its Diana opponent in the finals, all four Irelia decks in the Top 8, alongside the best Darius all were playing gear removal in their main deck. As a result, the best list featuring [Dazzling Aurora](https://riftbound.gg/cards/ogn-160-dazzling-aurora) is a Miss Fortune in the top 32, quite a setback after Sivir reached the top 4 in both regional tournaments last week.

This also affected most legends from the Body domain. Indeed, both Kha'zix and Origins Master Yi piloted a midrange build to the top 16, the best result for the domain in the Xi'an regional tournament. Some household names in the Aurora synergy also posted their best record without the gear in their list, such as Volibear. Last, Poppy, the surprise semi-finalist from the previous Chinese regional tournament, ended her best run in the top 256 in Xi'an.

In that context, the early discussion about the 9-cost gear might start to take a different turn. Early on, Master Yi looked like a dominant legend, battling with Irelia to be considered the best in Riftbound. After they split the regional tournaments last week, Irelia added four top 8s to her resume, while Master Yi failed to reach that same threshold for the second time. If the Wuju Bladesman isn't in contention to be considered one of Riftbound's finest, the popularity of Dazzling Aurora in the early weeks might actually be due to a lack of raw power in the other synergies available within the domain.

Irelia is now anchored as the top legend in the game, while Vex, LeBlanc and Diana just posted their second top 8. These are solidifying their spot as Riftbound best performers in Unleashed, while the pack of legends able to shoot for a top cut is also forming. In that second group, Ezreal, Fiora or Azir are posting consistent results.

Most of the uncertainty is around the Body Legends. Is Master Yi a guaranteed top tier perfomer, or should we worry after two top 16 out of three regional tournaments? Are Aurora decks threats until we start running cards to stop them, or can those decks adapt and fight back against [Salvage](https://riftbound.gg/cards/ogn-224-salvage), [Turn to Dust](https://riftbound.gg/cards/unl-070-turn-to-dust) or [Adaptatron](https://riftbound.gg/cards/ogn-056-adaptatron)?

Those with the answer to those final questions about the Unleashed metagame will definately have an edge next week in Vancouver. The others will likely play Irelia or another legend without too much uncertainty around it.

##### Den

Den has been in love with strategy games for as long as he can remember, starting with the Heroes of Might and Magic series as a kid. Card games came around the middle school - Yu-Gi-Oh! and then Magic: The Gathering.

Hearthstone and Legends of Runeterra has been his real breakthrough and he has been a coach, writer, and caster on the French scene for many years now. He now coaches aspiring pro players and writes various articles on these games.

[Articles: 86](https://riftbound.gg/author/denhs/)
